## user

Tool result:
import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { completeConditionItems, equivalenceItems, transferItems, confirmationItemsByError, obligationConfirmationItem } from './mixedDrillItems';
import { openingActivityItems } from './openingActivityItems';
import { mixedFamilyItems } from '../components/MixedFamilyPractice';

const baseline = [...completeConditionItems, ...equivalenceItems, ...transferItems];
const confirmations = Object.values(confirmationItemsByError).flat();

it('provides 24 baseline questions while keeping fresh confirmations separate and feedback complete', () => {
  expect(baseline.length + openingActivityItems.length + mixedFamilyItems.length).toBe(24);
  const all = [...baseline, ...confirmations];
  expect(new Set(all.map(item => item.id)).size).toBe(all.length);
  for (const item of all) {
    expect(item.correct).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    expect(item.correct).toBeLessThan(item.options.length);
    expect(item.feedback).toHaveLength(item.options.length);
    expect(new Set(item.options).size).toBe(item.options.length);
    for (const explanation of item.feedback) expect(explanation.trim().length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
    expect(item.sentence + item.prompt + item.options.join(' ') + item.feedback.join(' ')).not.toMatch(/[—–]|contrapositive|unless/i);
  }
});

it('confirms each repaired skill on a distinct fresh case', () => {
  expect(Object.keys(confirmationItemsByError).sort()).toEqual(['direction', 'equivalence', 'qualification', 'requirement']);
  for (const [skill, items] of Object.entries(confirmationItemsByError)) {
    expect(items.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const item of items) {
      expect(item.skill).toBe(skill);
      expect(item.stage).toBe('confirmation');
      expect(baseline.map(baselineItem => baselineItem.sentence)).not.toContain(item.sentence);
      expect(mixedFamilyItems.map(baselineItem => baselineItem.sentence)).not.toContain(item.sentence);
    }
  }
});

it('does not turn a satisfied permission requirement into granted permission or a required action into compliance', () => {
  const permission = transferItems.find(item => item.id === 'mixed-transfer-3')!;
  expect(permission.options[permission.correct]).toMatch(/permission remains open/i);
  expect(permission.feedback[permission.correct]).toMatch(/denied permission.*with permission/);
  const obligation = transferItems.find(item => item.id === 'mixed-transfer-4')!;
  expect(obligation.options[obligation.correct]).toMatch(/attendance instead of an obligation/i);
  expect(obligation.feedback[obligation.correct]).toMatch(/has not attended.*obligation/);
});

it('keeps the four reviewed envelope comparisons and identifies the if version as the reverse', () => {
  const same = equivalenceItems.map(item => item.options[item.correct] === 'Same rule');
  expect(same).toEqual([true, false, true, true]);
  expect(equivalenceItems[1].sentence).toMatch(/tray if it is stamped/);
  expect(equivalenceItems[3].sentence).toMatch(/tray only if it is stamped/);
});


it('diagnoses the chosen error rather than tagging every answer with the exercise skill', () => {
  for (const item of [...baseline, ...confirmations]) {
    expect(item.errorByOption).toHaveLength(item.options.length);
    expect(item.errorByOption?.[item.correct]).toBeNull();
    item.errorByOption?.forEach((error, index) => {
      if (index !== item.correct) expect(error).not.toBeNull();
    });
  }
  const swimmer = completeConditionItems[0];
  expect(swimmer.options[0]).toBe('Every swimmer');
  expect(swimmer.errorByOption?.[0]).toBe('qualification');
  expect(swimmer.options[2]).toBe('Anyone who receives a wristband');
  expect(swimmer.errorByOption?.[2]).toBe('direction');
  const trainee = transferItems.find(item => item.id === 'mixed-transfer-2')!;
  expect(trainee.errorByOption).toEqual(['qualification', 'direction', null]);
});


it('offers a fresh obligation confirmation that still leaves actual compliance open', () => {
  expect(obligationConfirmationItem.id).not.toBe('mixed-transfer-4');
  expect(obligationConfirmationItem.options[obligationConfirmationItem.correct]).toMatch(/required to bring/);
  expect(obligationConfirmationItem.feedback[obligationConfirmationItem.correct]).toMatch(/actually brought it remains open/);
  expect(obligationConfirmationItem.errorByOption?.[obligationConfirmationItem.correct]).toBeNull();
  expect(baseline.map(item => item.sentence)).not.toContain(obligationConfirmationItem.sentence);
});
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { StrictMode } from 'react';
import { afterEach, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { OpeningMixedDrill, openingDiagramItems } from './OpeningMixedDrill';
import { completeConditionItems, equivalenceItems, transferItems, confirmationItemsByError, obligationConfirmationItem } from '../content/mixedDrillItems';
import { mixedFamilyItems } from './MixedFamilyPractice';

afterEach(() => { cleanup(); vi.restoreAllMocks(); });
const active = () => within(document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-mixed-question]:not([hidden])')!);
const next = () => {
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /^(Next question|Try a fresh case|Review practice|Finish practice)$/ }));
  const proceed = screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Continue' });
  if (proceed) fireEvent.click(proceed);
};
function renderPractice() {
  const view = render(<OpeningMixedDrill/>);
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Begin practice' }));
  return view;
}
function choose(option: string) {
  fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: option }));
  fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Check answer' }));
}
function solve(index: number) {
  if (index < 3) return choose(completeConditionItems[index].options[completeConditionItems[index].correct]);
  if (index < 10) {
    const item = openingDiagramItems[index - 3];
    fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Choose the left condition' }));
    fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: item.left }));
    fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Choose the right condition' }));
    fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: item.right }));
    return fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Check answer' }));
  }
  if (index < 16) {
    const item = mixedFamilyItems[index - 10];
    fireEvent.change(active().getByLabelText('Your diagram'), { target: { value: `${item.left} → ${item.right}` } });
    fireEvent.change(active().getByLabelText('Read it in English'), { target: { value: item.readback } });
    fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Compare with the choices' }));
    fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: item.target }));
    fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: item.left }));
    return fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Check answer' }));
  }
  const item = index < 20 ? equivalenceItems[index - 16] : transferItems[index - 20];
  choose(item.options[item.correct]);
}

it('shows one task, a single 24-question counter, progress and predictable heading focus', () => {
  render(<StrictMode><OpeningMixedDrill/></StrictMode>);
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'What does the rule cover?' })).not.toHaveFocus();
  expect(screen.queryByRole('group', { name: 'Answer choices' })).toBeNull();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Begin practice' }));
  expect(screen.getByText('Question 1 of 24')).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'What does the rule cover?' })).toHaveFocus();
  expect(screen.getByRole('progressbar')).toHaveAttribute('aria-valuenow', '0');
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' })).toBeDisabled();
  expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Choose the left condition' })).toBeNull();
  choose(completeConditionItems[0].options[0]);
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' })).toBeDisabled();
  expect(screen.getByText(completeConditionItems[0].feedback[0])).toBeVisible();
  choose(completeConditionItems[0].options[completeConditionItems[0].correct]);
  expect(screen.getByRole('progressbar')).toHaveAttribute('aria-valuenow', '1');
  next();
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'What does the rule cover?' })).toHaveFocus();
  expect(screen.getByText('Question 2 of 24')).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByText(completeConditionItems[0].sentence)).not.toBeVisible();
});

it('reuses the real reviewed diagram engine without its own count, storage or untaught transformations', () => {
  const read = vi.spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'getItem');
  const write = vi.spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'setItem');
  renderPractice();
  for (let index = 0; index < 3; index++) { solve(index); next(); }
  expect(screen.getByText('Question 4 of 24')).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByText(openingDiagramItems[0].prompt)).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByText(/Statement 1 of|Sentence 1 of|Contrapositive/i)).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /^Not / })).toBeNull();
  solve(3);
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' })).toBeEnabled();
  next();
  expect(screen.getByText(openingDiagramItems[1].prompt)).toBeVisible();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Previous question' }));
  expect(active().getByRole('status')).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' })).toBeEnabled();
  expect(read).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  expect(write).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
});

it('keeps independent writing before supplied labels and retains it across questions', () => {
  renderPractice();
  for (let index = 0; index < 10; index++) { solve(index); next(); }
  expect(screen.getByText('Question 11 of 24')).toBeVisible();
  expect(active().queryByRole('button', { name: mixedFamilyItems[0].target })).toBeNull();
  expect(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Compare with the choices' })).toBeDisabled();
  solve(10);
  expect(active().getByText(`Read it back: ${mixedFamilyItems[0].readback}`)).toBeVisible();
  next();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Previous question' }));
  expect(active().getByLabelText('Read it in English')).toHaveValue(mixedFamilyItems[0].readback);
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' })).toBeEnabled();
});

it('freezes first checks and treats a repaired fresh answer differently from an independent confirmation', () => {
  renderPractice();
  choose(completeConditionItems[0].options[0]);
  for (let index = 0; index < 24; index++) { solve(index); next(); }
  const fresh = confirmationItemsByError.qualification[0];
  expect(screen.getByText('24 main questions completed')).toBeVisible();

  expect(screen.getByText(fresh.sentence)).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByText(completeConditionItems[0].sentence)).not.toBeVisible();
  choose(fresh.options[(fresh.correct + 1) % fresh.options.length]);
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Finish practice' })).toBeDisabled();
  choose(fresh.options[fresh.correct]);
  next();
  expect(screen.getByText('You answered 23 of 24 correctly on your first try. You corrected 1 after checking the explanation.')).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByText(/Who or what the rule covers: you corrected the new question after checking the explanation/)).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByText(/you answered the new question correctly on your first try/)).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.getByText(/Your written translations are for you to compare with the examples/)).toBeVisible();
  expect(document.querySelector('[data-mixed-question="mixed-independent-1"] textarea')).toHaveValue(`${mixedFamilyItems[0].left} → ${mixedFamilyItems[0].right}`);
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Review my answers' }));
  for (let index = 0; index < 10; index++) next();
  expect(active().getByLabelText('Your diagram')).toHaveValue(`${mixedFamilyItems[0].left} → ${mixedFamilyItems[0].right}`);
  expect(active().getByLabelText('Read it in English')).toHaveValue(mixedFamilyItems[0].readback);
  expect(active().getByText(`Read it back: ${mixedFamilyItems[0].readback}`)).toBeVisible();
}, 30000);

it('groups a wrong role choice with a new direction question instead of a qualification question', () => {
  renderPractice();

});

  renderPractice();

  fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Mia trains' }));
  fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Choose the right condition' }));

  fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' }));
  for (let index = 3; index < 24; index++) { solve(index); next(); }

  expect(screen.queryByText(confirmationItemsByError.qualification[0].sentence)).toBeNull();
});

/**
 * Instruction-page walkthroughs, one per drill. Each step mirrors a step the
 * drill actually asks for, in the same order, using an example sentence that
 * is not one of the drill's own questions.
 */
export type DemoFrame =
  | { kind: 'pick'; words: string[]; picks: number[]; chip: string }
  | { kind: 'type'; lines: { label?: string; text: string }[] }
  | { kind: 'place'; left: string; right: string }
  | { kind: 'choose'; question: string; options: string[]; correct: number };

export type DemoStep = { title: string; caption: string; frame: DemoFrame };
export type DrillDemo = { sentence?: string; steps: DemoStep[] };

const words = (sentence: string) => sentence.split(' ');

function contrapositiveDemo(sentence: string, picks: number[], chip: string, one: string, two: string, s1: string, s2: string, ifThen: string, contraWords: string): DrillDemo {
  return { sentence, steps: [
    { title: 'Find the indicators', caption: 'Click each indicator word, then choose its group.', frame: { kind: 'pick', words: words(sentence), picks, chip } },
    { title: 'Keep the complete ideas', caption: 'Click the first and last word of each condition. Leave the indicator out.', frame: { kind: 'pick', words: words(sentence), picks: words(sentence).map((_, i) => i).filter(i => !picks.includes(i)), chip: 'Both conditions marked' } },
    { title: 'Define your symbols', caption: 'Give each condition its own letter, and reuse it.', frame: { kind: 'type', lines: [{ text: `${s1} = ${one.toLowerCase()}` }, { text: `${s2} = ${two.toLowerCase()}` }] } },
    { title: 'Build the diagram', caption: 'Put your letters in the slots: what is enough on the left, what it requires on the right.', frame: { kind: 'type', lines: [{ label: 'Your diagram', text: `${s1} → ${s2}` }] } },
    { title: 'Reverse and negate', caption: 'Switch the sides, then turn NOT on for both.', frame: { kind: 'type', lines: [{ label: 'Original', text: `${s1} → ${s2}` }, { label: 'Contrapositive', text: `NOT ${s2} → NOT ${s1}` }] } },
    { title: 'Read the original', caption: 'Write the original rule as an if/then sentence.', frame: { kind: 'type', lines: [{ text: ifThen }] } },
    { title: 'Read the contrapositive', caption: 'Write the contrapositive in plain words.', frame: { kind: 'type', lines: [{ text: contraWords }] } },
  ] };
}

export const drillDemos: Record<string, DrillDemo> = {
  'foundation-practice': { steps: [
    { title: 'Rules and facts', caption: 'Decide what a rule and a fact prove together.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'Rule: If the store is open, its lights are on. Fact: The store is open. What follows?', options: ['The lights are on.', 'Not settled.'], correct: 0 } },
    { title: 'Sufficient and necessary', caption: 'Decide which condition is enough and which is required.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'If the store is open, its lights are on. Which condition is sufficient?', options: ['The store is open', 'The lights are on'], correct: 0 } },
    { title: 'Indicator words', caption: 'Find the word that tells you the rule is conditional.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'Only staff may enter the kitchen. Which word is the indicator?', options: ['staff', 'Only', 'enter'], correct: 1 } },
  ] },
  'sufficient-practice': { sentence: 'Any bike left overnight is moved to the shed.', steps: [
    { title: 'Find the indicator', caption: 'Choose the word that introduces what is enough.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'Which indicator introduces the sufficient condition?', options: ['moved', 'Any', 'shed'], correct: 1 } },
    { title: 'Find the condition', caption: 'Choose the complete condition the indicator points to.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'What complete condition does “Any” identify?', options: ['A bike', 'A bike is left overnight'], correct: 1 } },
    { title: 'Build the diagram', caption: 'Choose the direction that keeps the rule.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'Which diagram keeps the rule?', options: ['Left overnight → Moved to shed', 'Moved to shed → Left overnight'], correct: 0 } },
    { title: 'Write it in English', caption: 'Write your diagram as an if/then sentence, then compare it with ours.', frame: { kind: 'type', lines: [{ label: 'Your if/then sentence', text: 'If a bike is left overnight, then it is moved to the shed.' }] } },
  ] },
  'contrapositive-practice': { sentence: 'If the oven is on, the fan runs.', steps: [
    { title: 'Original rule', caption: 'Choose the diagram that keeps the original rule.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'Which original diagram keeps the complete conditions?', options: ['Oven on → Fan runs', 'Fan runs → Oven on'], correct: 0 } },
    { title: 'Reverse and negate', caption: 'Choose the contrapositive: switch the sides and negate both.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'Which diagram is the contrapositive?', options: ['Fan runs → Oven on', 'NOT (Fan runs) → NOT (Oven on)'], correct: 1 } },
    { title: 'Write it in English', caption: 'Write the contrapositive in plain words.', frame: { kind: 'type', lines: [{ text: 'If the fan does not run, then the oven is not on.' }] } },
  ] },
  'mixed-practice': { steps: [
    { title: 'What does the rule cover?', caption: 'Keep the words that limit who the rule covers.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'Every runner wearing a red bib gets water first. Who does the rule cover?', options: ['Every runner', 'Runners wearing a red bib'], correct: 1 } },
    { title: 'Build the diagram', caption: 'Put the condition that is enough on the left.', frame: { kind: 'place', left: 'Runner wears a red bib', right: 'Gets water first' } },
    { title: 'Translate it yourself', caption: 'Write your own diagram before seeing ours.', frame: { kind: 'type', lines: [{ label: 'Your diagram', text: 'Red bib → Water first' }] } },
    { title: 'Does the meaning change?', caption: 'Compare two wordings and decide whether they say the same thing.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'Does “Only runners in red bibs get water first” say the same thing?', options: ['Yes, same rule', 'No, it changes the rule'], correct: 1 } },
    { title: 'Try unfamiliar cases', caption: 'Use the same reasoning on new wording.', frame: { kind: 'choose', question: 'Guests must sign in. What does this tell you?', options: ['Every guest did sign in', 'Signing in is required of every guest'], correct: 1 } },
  ] },
  'source-intro-identify': { sentence: 'You can board only if you have a ticket.', steps: [
    { title: 'Find the indicators', caption: 'Click each word of the indicator, then choose its group.', frame: { kind: 'pick', words: words('You can board only if you have a ticket.'), picks: [3, 4], chip: 'Group 2: necessary' } },
    { title: 'Keep the complete ideas', caption: 'Click the first and last word of each condition. Leave the indicator out.', frame: { kind: 'pick', words: words('You can board only if you have a ticket.'), picks: [0, 1, 2, 5, 6, 7, 8], chip: 'Both conditions marked' } },
  ] },
  'source-roles': { sentence: 'Every member who pays dues can vote.', steps: [
    { title: 'Keep the complete ideas', caption: 'Click the first and last word of each condition. Leave the indicator out.', frame: { kind: 'pick', words: words('Every member who pays dues can vote.'), picks: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], chip: 'Both conditions marked' } },

    { title: 'Define your symbols', caption: 'Give each condition its own letter.', frame: { kind: 'type', lines: [{ text: 'O = the store is open' }, { text: 'L = the lights are on' }] } },

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { drillDemos, type DemoFrame } from '../content/drillDemos';
import './drill-demo.css';

const STEP_MS = 5200;

function reducedMotion() {
  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && !!window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
}

function Frame({ frame }: { frame: DemoFrame }) {
  if (frame.kind === 'pick') {
    let order = 0;
    return <div className="dd-pick">
      <p className="dd-sentence">{frame.words.map((word, index) => {
        const picked = frame.picks.includes(index);
        const delay = picked ? 0.7 + 0.7 * order++ : 0;
        return <span key={index} className={picked ? 'dd-word dd-picked' : 'dd-word'} style={picked ? { animationDelay: `${delay}s` } : undefined}>{word}</span>;
      })}</p>
      <span className="dd-chip" style={{ animationDelay: `${0.9 + 0.7 * frame.picks.length}s` }}>{frame.chip}</span>
    </div>;
  }
  if (frame.kind === 'type') return <div className="dd-type">{frame.lines.map((line, index) => <div key={index} className="dd-line">
    {line.label && <span className="dd-label">{line.label}</span>}
    <span className="dd-field"><span className="dd-typed" style={{ animationDelay: `${0.4 + index * 1.4}s` }}>{line.text}</span></span>
  </div>)}</div>;
  if (frame.kind === 'place') return <div className="dd-place">
    <div className="dd-slot"><span className="dd-label">Enough (left)</span><span className="dd-card dd-card-left">{frame.left}</span></div>
    <span className="dd-arrow" aria-hidden="true">→</span>
    <div className="dd-slot"><span className="dd-label">Required (right)</span><span className="dd-card dd-card-right">{frame.right}</span></div>
  </div>;
  return <div className="dd-choose">
    <p className="dd-question">{frame.question}</p>
    <div className="dd-options">{frame.options.map((option, index) => <span key={index} className={index === frame.correct ? 'dd-option dd-correct' : 'dd-option'}>
      <i>{index === frame.correct ? <Check size={13}/> : String.fromCharCode(65 + index)}</i>{option}
    </span>)}</div>
  </div>;
}

/** Instruction-page walkthrough: one animated frame per step of this drill, in drill order. */
export function DrillDemo({ drillId }: { drillId: string }) {
  const demo = drillDemos[drillId];
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const [paused, setPaused] = useState(reducedMotion);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!demo || paused) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setIndex(current => (current + 1) % demo.steps.length), STEP_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [demo, index, paused]);
  if (!demo) return null;
  const step = demo.steps[index];
  return <section className="dd-demo" aria-label="How this drill works">
    <p className="dd-eyebrow">How this drill works</p>
    {demo.sentence && <blockquote className="dd-example">Example: {demo.sentence}</blockquote>}
    <ol className="dd-steps">{demo.steps.map((item, i) => <li key={item.title}>
      <button type="button" aria-current={i === index ? 'step' : undefined} onClick={() => { setIndex(i); setPaused(true); }}><span>{i + 1}</span>{item.title}</button>
    </li>)}</ol>
    <div className="dd-stage" key={index} aria-live="polite">
      <p className="dd-caption"><strong>Step {index + 1}.</strong> {step.caption}</p>
      <Frame frame={step.frame}/>
    </div>
    {demo.steps.length > 1 && <div className="dd-progress" aria-hidden="true"><i key={`${index}-${paused}`} className={paused ? 'dd-bar-paused' : ''} style={{ animationDuration: `${STEP_MS}ms` }}/></div>}
  </section>;
}

import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';
import { StagedReasoningDrill, sufficientStagedItems, contrapositiveStagedItems } from './StagedReasoningDrill';
import { diagramExamples, flashcardExamples, translationExamples } from './ConditionalOpeningLesson';
import { referencePracticeItems } from './ConditionalReferenceSequence';
afterEach(cleanup);
beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear());
const mount = (mode: 'sufficient' | 'contrapositive') => render(<MemoryRouter><StagedReasoningDrill mode={mode}/></MemoryRouter>);

it('uses one fresh sentence per Group 1 indicator, none copied from a lesson, plus three practice items', () => {
  const lessons = [...translationExamples['group-1'], ...diagramExamples['group-1'], ...flashcardExamples['group-1']].map(item => item.sentence);
  expect(sufficientStagedItems).toHaveLength(13);
  sufficientStagedItems.forEach(item => expect(lessons).not.toContain(item.sentence));
  expect(sufficientStagedItems.map(item => item.sentence)).not.toContain('A report is reviewed if it is submitted before noon.');
  expect(sufficientStagedItems.map(item => item.sentence)).not.toContain('The only notebooks kept on this shelf are hardback.');
  const indicatorItems = sufficientStagedItems.slice(0, 10);
  expect(indicatorItems.map(item => item.stages[0].options![item.stages[0].correct!])).toEqual(['if', 'when', 'whenever', 'where', 'all', 'every', 'each', 'any', 'the only', 'people who']);
  // Distractors vary from item to item instead of always offering only if and requires.
  const distractors = indicatorItems.map(item => item.stages[0].options!.filter((_, i) => i !== item.stages[0].correct).join('|'));
  expect(new Set(distractors).size).toBe(indicatorItems.length);
  expect(new Set(indicatorItems.map(item => item.stages[0].correct)).size).toBe(3);
  for (const item of indicatorItems) {
    const [indicator, , , write] = item.stages;
    indicator.options!.forEach((_, i) => { if (i !== indicator.correct) expect(indicator.hints![i]).toBeTruthy(); });
    expect(write).toMatchObject({ name: 'Write it in English', prompt: 'Write your diagram as an if/then sentence.', label: 'Your if/then sentence' });
    expect(write.model).toMatch(/^If .*, then /);
  }
  expect(contrapositiveStagedItems).toHaveLength(9);
  referencePracticeItems.forEach((item, i) => {
    const stages = contrapositiveStagedItems[i].stages;
    expect(stages[stages.length - 1].model).toBe(item.contraReadback);
    // A sentence already written as if/then is not copied back word for word.
    expect(stages.some(stage => stage.name === 'Write it in English')).toBe(item.readback !== item.sentence);
  });
  expect(contrapositiveStagedItems[0].stages).toHaveLength(3);
  expect(contrapositiveStagedItems[1].stages).toHaveLength(4);
});

it('shows one stage at a time and requires a correct checked choice', () => {
  mount('sufficient');
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' })).toBeDisabled();
  expect(screen.queryByRole('textbox')).toBeNull();
  const options = within(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Answer choices' })).getAllByRole('button');
  fireEvent.click(options[1]);
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Check this step' }));
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Not quite. Try again.' })).toHaveFocus();
  expect(screen.getByText(/“only if” introduces what is required, not what is enough/)).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByText(/with the complete condition it refers to/)).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' })).toBeDisabled();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' }));
  fireEvent.click(options[0]);
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Check this step' }));
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' }));
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: /What complete condition/ })).toHaveFocus();
  expect(screen.getByRole('progressbar')).toHaveAttribute('aria-valuenow', '0');
});

it('requires an attempted written readback and explicit self-comparison', () => {
  localStorage.setItem('conditional-staged-v1:contrapositive', JSON.stringify({ index: 1, step: 0, answers: {}, fingerprint: JSON.stringify(contrapositiveStagedItems) }));
  mount('contrapositive');
  for (const correct of [1, 0]) {
    fireEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Answer choices' })).getAllByRole('button')[correct]);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Check this step' }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' }));
  }
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Write your diagram as an if/then sentence.' })).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Your if/then sentence' })).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Compare my sentence' })).toBeDisabled();
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole('textbox'), { target: { value: 'My reading.' } });
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Compare my sentence' }));
  expect(screen.getByText('Our version:')).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' })).toBeDisabled();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'I compared the direction and complete conditions' }));
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' }));
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Now write the contrapositive in ordinary English.' })).toBeVisible();
});

it('skips the English step when the rule is already an if/then sentence', () => {
  mount('contrapositive');
  for (const correct of [0, 1]) {
    fireEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Answer choices' })).getAllByRole('button')[correct]);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Check this step' }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' }));
  }
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Now write the contrapositive in ordinary English.' })).toBeVisible();
});

it('keeps answers saved against an older item set in the record instead of reading them against new sentences', () => {
  const answers = { '0:0': { choice: 2, writing: '', checked: true, accepted: true }, '0:3': { choice: null, writing: 'Old readback.', checked: true, accepted: true } };
  localStorage.setItem('conditional-staged-v1:sufficient', JSON.stringify({ index: 0, step: 3, answers, fingerprint: 'v2:old' }));
  mount('sufficient');
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Which indicator introduces the sufficient condition?' })).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Check this step' })).toBeDisabled();
  const stored = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('conditional-staged-v1:sufficient')!);
  expect(stored.answers).toEqual({});
  expect(stored.archived).toEqual([{ fingerprint: 'v2:old', index: 0, step: 3, answers }]);
});

it('restores drafts and invalidates later checks when an earlier step is revised', () => {
  const answers = { '0:0': { choice: 0, writing: '', checked: true, accepted: true }, '0:1': { choice: 1, writing: '', checked: true, accepted: true }, '0:2': { choice: null, writing: 'My saved reasoning.', checked: true, accepted: true } };
  localStorage.setItem('conditional-staged-v1:contrapositive', JSON.stringify({ index: 0, step: 0, answers, fingerprint: JSON.stringify(contrapositiveStagedItems) }));
  mount('contrapositive');
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Right rule.' })).toBeVisible();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Revise my answer' }));
  const stored = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('conditional-staged-v1:contrapositive')!);
  expect(stored.answers['0:1'].accepted).toBe(false);
  expect(stored.answers['0:2'].checked).toBe(false);
  expect(stored.answers['0:2'].writing).toBe('My saved reasoning.');
});

import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';
import { OpeningCourseContent } from './OpeningCourseContent';
import { DrillOneEntry } from './DrillOneExperience';
import { CourseProgressProvider, type CourseProgressStore } from '../courseProgress';

afterEach(cleanup);

// Rule-vs-fact questions first, then the concept checks, then indicators.
const correctAnswers = [1, 2, 0, 0, 2, 0, 1, 0, 2, 1, 2, 0, 1, 0, 2, 1, 1, 0, 2, 1, 0, 2, 1, 2];
const answerOptions = () => within(screen.getByRole('group')).getAllByRole('button');
const check = () => fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Check answer' }));

it('requires a deliberate check before feedback, diagrams, progress or forward navigation', () => {
  render(<OpeningCourseContent id="foundation-practice"/>);
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Does the rule itself tell us that you actually win the raffle?' })).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByRole('progressbar')).toHaveAttribute('aria-valuenow', '0');
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Check answer' })).toBeDisabled();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' })).toBeDisabled();
  expect(screen.queryByText('Read the rule left to right')).toBeNull();
  fireEvent.click(answerOptions()[1]);
  expect(screen.queryByText('Correct.')).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' })).toBeDisabled();
  expect(screen.getByRole('progressbar')).toHaveAttribute('aria-valuenow', '0');
  check();
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Correct.' })).toHaveFocus();
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Correct.' })).toHaveAccessibleDescription(/Imagining a win helps/);
  expect(screen.getByText('Read the rule left to right')).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' })).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.getByRole('progressbar')).toHaveAttribute('aria-valuenow', '1');
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' })).toBeEnabled();
});

it('gives a hint for a wrong answer without marking or naming the correct option', () => {
  render(<OpeningCourseContent id="foundation-practice"/>);
  fireEvent.click(answerOptions()[0]);
  check();
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Not quite.' })).toHaveFocus();
  expect(screen.getByText(/You added a case fact/)).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByText(/The correct answer is marked above/)).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.queryByText('Correct answer')).toBeNull();
  expect(answerOptions().filter(option => option.getAttribute('data-correct') === 'true')).toHaveLength(0);
  expect(answerOptions()[0]).toHaveAttribute('data-incorrect', 'true');
  expect(screen.queryByText('Read the rule left to right')).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' })).toBeDisabled();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' })).toBeVisible();
});

it('retains the first answer through repair and restores focus to an enabled choice', () => {
  render(<OpeningCourseContent id="foundation-practice"/>);
  fireEvent.click(answerOptions()[0]);
  check();
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Not quite.' })).toHaveFocus();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' }));
  expect(answerOptions()[0]).toHaveFocus();
  expect(answerOptions()[0]).toBeEnabled();
  expect(screen.queryByText('Not quite.')).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' })).toBeDisabled();
  expect(screen.getByRole('progressbar')).toHaveAttribute('aria-valuenow', '1');
  fireEvent.click(answerOptions()[1]);
  check();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' }));
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: /It's Friday/ })).toHaveFocus();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Previous question' }));
  expect(answerOptions()[1]).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Correct.' })).toBeVisible();
  for (let index = 1; index < correctAnswers.length; index++) {
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' }));
    fireEvent.click(answerOptions()[correctAnswers[index]]);
    check();
  }
  expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Back to results' })).toBeNull();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'See results' }));
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Spot the condition complete.' })).toHaveFocus();
  expect(screen.getByText(/of 24 correct on your first attempt/)).toHaveTextContent('23 of 24');
  expect(screen.getByRole('progressbar')).toHaveAttribute('aria-valuenow', '24');
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Question 1 Review reasoning' }));
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: /actually win the raffle/ })).toHaveFocus();
  expect(answerOptions()[1]).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' })).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Back to results' })).toBeVisible();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Back to results' }));
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Spot the condition complete.' })).toHaveFocus();
  expect(screen.getByText(/of 24 correct on your first attempt/)).toHaveTextContent('23 of 24');
});

it('checks every option across all 24 questions in the rule, concept, indicator order', () => {
  render(<OpeningCourseContent id="foundation-practice"/>);
  for (let index = 0; index < correctAnswers.length; index++) {
    expect(screen.getByText(`Question ${index + 1} of 24`)).toBeVisible();
    const choices = answerOptions();
    const explanations = new Set<string>();
    choices.forEach((choice, option) => {
      if (option === correctAnswers[index]) return;
      fireEvent.click(choice);
      check();
      expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Not quite.' })).toBeVisible();
      explanations.add(document.querySelector('.d1-feedback p')?.textContent || '');
      fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' }));
    });
    fireEvent.click(choices[correctAnswers[index]]);
    check();
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Correct.' })).toBeVisible();
    explanations.add(document.querySelector('.d1-feedback p')?.textContent || '');
    expect(explanations.size).toBe(choices.length);
    if (index === 5) expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'What is sufficient here?' })).toBeVisible();
    if (index === 6) expect(screen.getByText('If you win the raffle, you get a bicycle.')).toBeVisible();
    if (index === 7) expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'What is a necessary condition?' })).toBeVisible();
    if (index < 23) fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' }));
  }
  expect(screen.getByText('Every completed registration requires a paid deposit.')).toBeVisible();
});

it('keeps every saved answer with its question when the question order changed', () => {
  // Order 1 (no version): 17 concept and indicator questions, then the 7 rule-vs-fact questions.
  const legacy = Array.from({ length: 24 }, () => ({ selected: null as number | null, checked: false, first: null as number | null }));
  legacy[0] = { selected: 1, checked: true, first: 1 }; // "What is a necessary condition?": a wrong answer
  legacy[17] = { selected: 1, checked: true, first: 1 }; // the raffle question: the right answer
  let steps: CourseProgressStore['steps'] = { 'foundation-practice': { status: 'started', updatedAt: '2026-10-10T00:00:00.000Z', state: { current: 17, answers: legacy, finished: false, seenResults: false } } };
  const store: CourseProgressStore = { steps, save: (id, patch) => { steps = { ...steps, [id]: { ...steps[id], ...patch, status: steps[id]?.status === 'done' ? 'done' : patch.status ?? steps[id]?.status ?? 'started', updatedAt: 'now' } }; } };
  render(<CourseProgressProvider store={store}><OpeningCourseContent id="foundation-practice"/></CourseProgressProvider>);
  expect(screen.getByText('Question 1 of 24')).toBeVisible();
  expect(answerOptions()[1]).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Correct.' })).toBeVisible();
  const saved = steps['foundation-practice'].state as { order: number; current: number; answers: typeof legacy };
  expect(saved.order).toBe(2);
  expect(saved.current).toBe(0);
  expect(saved.answers[0]).toEqual(legacy[17]);
  expect(saved.answers[7]).toEqual(legacy[0]);
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' }));
  for (let index = 1; index < 7; index++) {
    fireEvent.click(answerOptions()[correctAnswers[index]]);
    check();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' }));
  }
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'What is a necessary condition?' })).toBeVisible();
  expect(answerOptions()[1]).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Not quite.' })).toBeVisible();
});

it('titles the entry page Spot the condition', () => {
  render(<MemoryRouter><DrillOneEntry startUrl="/start" lessonsUrl="/lessons"/></MemoryRouter>);
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Spot the condition' })).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByText(/Drill/)).toBeNull();
  expect(document.body.textContent).not.toMatch(/trigger|required outcome/i);
});

});

it('checks an obligation mistake with a new obligation case rather than a different equivalence skill', () => {
  renderPractice();
  for (let index = 0; index < 23; index++) { solve(index); next(); }
  choose(transferItems[3].options[0]);
  solve(23);
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Try a fresh case' })).toBeEnabled();
  expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Review practice' })).toBeNull();
  next();
  expect(screen.getByText(obligationConfirmationItem.sentence)).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByText(confirmationItemsByError.equivalence[0].sentence)).toBeNull();
  choose(obligationConfirmationItem.options[obligationConfirmationItem.correct]);
  next();
  expect(screen.getByText(/What is required and what actually happened: you answered the new question correctly on your first try/)).toBeVisible();
});

it('pauses before a new stage with its own explanation instead of stacking an overview above every task', () => {
  renderPractice();
  for (let index = 0; index < 2; index++) { solve(index); next(); }
  solve(2);
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next question' }));
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Build the diagram' })).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByText('If a parcel has a blue sticker, it has been inspected.')).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Choose the left condition' })).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.queryByRole('progressbar')).toBeNull();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue' }));
  expect(screen.getByText('Question 4 of 24')).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Choose the left condition' })).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.getByText('If a parcel has a blue sticker, it has been inspected.')).not.toBeVisible();
});

it('offers review directly when no first-attempt errors need a fresh case', () => {
  renderPractice();
  for (let index = 0; index < 23; index++) { solve(index); next(); }
  solve(23);
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Review practice' })).toBeEnabled();
  expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Try a fresh case' })).toBeNull();
  next();
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Review your practice' })).toBeVisible();
  expect(screen.queryByRole('heading', { name: 'How the new sentences went' })).toBeNull();
}, 30000);

it('returns focus to the first answer when an incorrect choice is retried', () => {
  renderPractice();
  choose(completeConditionItems[0].options[0]);
  const retry = active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Try this question again' });
  retry.focus();
  fireEvent.click(retry);
  expect(active().getByRole('button', { name: completeConditionItems[0].options[0] })).toHaveFocus();
  expect(active().queryByRole('button', { name: 'Try this question again' })).toBeNull();
  expect(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Check answer' })).toBeDisabled();
});

it('builds diagrams from full condition labels and gives only a hint for a reversed arrow', () => {
  renderPractice();
  for (let index = 0; index < 3; index++) { solve(index); next(); }
  expect(active().queryByRole('button', { name: 'trains' })).toBeNull();
  fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Mia qualifies' }));
  fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Choose the right condition' }));
  fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Mia trains' }));
  fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Check answer' }));
  const diagram = document.querySelector('[data-mixed-question]:not([hidden]) .omd-diagram')!;
  expect(diagram).toHaveAttribute('data-result', 'wrong');
  const feedback = diagram.querySelector('.cir-feedback')!;
  expect(feedback.querySelector('p')).toHaveTextContent('Check which condition is enough to prove the other.');
  expect(feedback.textContent).not.toMatch(/Training is enough|If Mia trains/);
  fireEvent.click(active().getByRole('button', { name: 'Try again' }));
  solve(3);
  expect(diagram).toHaveAttribute('data-result', 'right');
  expect(diagram.querySelector('.cir-feedback p')).toHaveTextContent('Right. Read it back: If Mia trains, she qualifies.');
  expect(diagram.querySelector('.cir-feedback')).toHaveTextContent(/Training is enough under this rule/);
});

it('maps the shared one-word diagram labels to full condition labels', () => {
  expect(openingDiagramItems.map(item => [item.left, item.right])).toEqual([
    ['Mia trains', 'Mia qualifies'], ['Person is a dancer', 'Person stretches'], ['Student is admitted', 'Student is a senior'],
    ['Person votes', 'Person is a member'], ['Bridge opens', 'Bridge has been inspected'], ['There is funding', 'There is approval'], ['Person is a hiker', 'Person brings a map'],
  ]);
});
